In the Last 24 Hours

Tolan 成功集資三千萬美元做佢嘅 AI 伴侶 app，而 Replika 創辦人 Eugenia Kuyda 亦正式推出咗 Wabi，一個多人協作嘅個人 AI 助手，係佢之前 Luka 同 Replika 嘅進化版。

Tenstorrent 估值衝到六十二億美元，要規模化開放權重 AI 晶片；Nvidia 就推出咗 Nemotron 3.

5 Super VL 同埋兩款輕量 Bolt 模型，話喺 agentic 任務上贏過 Anthropic 同 OpenAI。 Laplace Intelligence 用 AI 自動紅隊測試機械人同自動駕駛車，Anthropic 就承諾將百分之五嘅股份撥入「公共利益基金」做社會公益。 另外有人用 Claude Code 呢類 AI 編碼工具將智能電視變返「笨電視」，去掉廣告同追蹤功能；Apple 市場總監 Greg Joswiak 就話 AI 競賽入面最激進嗰班係矽谷嘅「瘋狂邊緣人」。

Apple 宣佈十月二十八號開「Pro and tell」發佈會，重頭戲係一款全新設計嘅 MacBook Pro，有 OLED 螢幕、觸控、Dynamic Island 同 M5 Pro/Max 晶片，仲有 A20 Pro 晶片 OLED 螢幕嘅 iPad mini、M6 晶片嘅 iMac 同入門版十四吋 MacBook Pro。

iPhone Duo 供貨緊張，交貨日期一度滑到明年一月，不過部分訂單又突然提早咗，作者話呢個情況其實係一把雙刃劍，主要因為鉸鏈良率唔高。

新出嘅 Apple TV 4K 賣二百九十九美元被大規模批評太貴，但 Amazon 就比 Apple 平仲快，AirPods Pro 3 仲有五十美元折扣。 Apple Watch Ultra 幫一個空軍退伍軍人發現咗佢自己唔知嘅

TechCrunch AI 20 articles

Last 24 Hours

TechCrunchAI's coverage over the past day centered on the accelerating race to build and fund AI infrastructure, with Tenstorrent raising $1.5 billion at a $6.2 billion valuation and Nvidia launching a new family of open-weight models for enterprise agents and local PCs. On the consumer side, the site highlighted AI companion startups like Tolan and Wabi gaining traction, while also noting a grassroots movement of users deploying AI tools to strip ads and surveillance features from smart TVs. Anthropic's pledge to commit 5% of its shares to a public benefit fund added a notable ethics dimension to an otherwise hardware-and-funding-heavy news cycle.

過去一日 TechCrunchAI 嘅報導主要集中喺 AI 基建嘅融資同產品發佈，Tenstorrent 以 62 億美元估值募到 15 億美元，Nvidia 亦推出咗一系列開源模型畀企業同本地 AI PC 用。消費端方面，AI 伴侶應用 Tolan 同 Wabi 相繼獲得關注，同時仲有網民用 AI 工具將智能電視入面嘅廣告同監控功能拆走，反映消費者開始用 AI 反擊科技巨頭。另外 Anthropic 宣布將 5% 股份撥入公共福利基金，喺一眾融資同硬件新聞入面算係幾有社會意義嘅一則。

The company, which raised $1.75 million in pre-seed funding backed by Reddit co-founder Alexis Ohanian, has already partnered with the ATHLOS track-and-field competition and embedded chips into LeBron James' first Nike shoes, offering brands a way to authenticate products and build digital ownership experiences for customers. As luxury counterfeits grow increasingly sophisticated, the move signals a shift toward embedding verification directly into the physical object itself.

Veritas 係一間由前 Tesla 產品經理 Luci Holland 創辦嘅初創公司，佢哋嘅防偽晶片技術已經由時裝擴展到珠寶、獎盃同香水樽，用嘅晶片每次拍一下都會生成獨特嘅安全回應，仲有防拆線，唔似 QR code 或者 NFC 咁容易被人複製。呢間公司已經籌到 175 萬美元嘅種子前資金，Reddit 聯合創辦人 Alexis Ohanian 都有投資，而家已經同 ATHLOS 田徑比賽合作，仲將晶片嵌入咗 LeBron James 第一對 Nike 鞋入面，俾品牌可以驗證產品同時為顧客建立數碼擁有權體驗。隨住奢侈品偽造技術越嚟越精，呢個趨勢顯示未來驗證會直接嵌入實體產品本身。

Introducing Clairic, a new System One class of extraction models

https://blog.cloudflare.com/clairic-extraction-model/📅 2026-10-20 01:48 UTC+8

Cloudflare has announced Clairic, a new class of System One extraction models that extract, compute, estimate, and forecast typed values from unstructured inputs in a single forward pass without generating any tokens. Unlike traditional LLMs, Clairic responds in under a second, charges only for input, and returns values with confidence scores and character offsets, making it significantly faster, cheaper, and more accurate for structured-output tasks like invoice parsing or forecasting. It fills a gap between decision models like Jev and generative LLMs, forming a three-tier system where extraction turns raw input into typed values, decision models turn values into choices, and LLMs turn decisions into language.

Cloudflare 推出咗一個叫 Clairic 嘅新模型，屬於 System One 提取模型，可以一次過從非結構化輸入入面提取、計算、估算同預測有類型嘅數值，完全唔需要生成 token。佢比傳統 LLM 快好多、平好多、準好多，因為佢唔會逐字生成，而係直接返轉頭帶住信心分數同字元位置嘅值，一秒內就搞掂。呢個模型同 Jev、Clef 呢啲決策模型一齊，填補咗快速決策同慢速生成推理之間嘅空隙，形成一個完整嘅三層架構。

The system uses deterministic code to gather evidence before any AI inference, then runs four specialist agents in parallel for traffic analysis, customer context, global telemetry, and threat intelligence, with an open-source model called Clef handling initial triage and a synthesis agent combining findings into a single advisory. This architecture was built after a single-agent prototype hallucinated unsupported claims, and the new approach ensures every recommendation is grounded in versioned, auditable evidence while preserving customer privacy through aggregate-only global signals.

Cloudflare 喺 Managed Defense 服務入面部署咗一套多 AI agent 安全營運系統，專登解決大量安全警報同時湧入、令分析師應接不暇嘅問題。佢哋用確定性程式碼先收集證據，再將流量分析、客戶背景、全球遙測同威脅情報四個專家 agent 並行運作，配合自家開源模型 Clef 做初步篩選，最後由綜合 agent 合併成一份建議。呢個架構係因為之前單一 agent 會幻覺出冇證據支持嘅結論，而家每個建議都要引用版本化嘅證據包，確保可審計同時保障客戶私隱。

The keys to the Internet change on October 11. Are you ready?

https://blog.cloudflare.com/root-ksk-2024-rollover/📅 2026-10-07 01:50 UTC+8

The DNS root key-signing key will undergo its second-ever rollover on October 11, 2026, replacing KSK-2017 with KSK-2024, and any DNSSEC-validating resolver that fails to trust the new key could render all websites unreachable for its users. Cloudflare has already added the new key to its built-in trust anchors and published a readiness test using the RFC 8509 sentinel protocol so users can verify their resolver trusts the replacement before the switch. Most website operators need take no action, but resolver administrators should confirm their systems have adopted the new anchor.

DNS 根金鑰將於2026年10月11號進行第二次滾動更新，由KSK-2017換成KSK-2024，如果DNSSEC驗證解析器冇信任新金鑰，用戶可能連所有網站都開唔到。Cloudflare已經將新金鑰加入內置信任錨點，仲推出咗一個測試網站，用RFC 8509協議等你可以提前確認自己部機有冇信任新金鑰。一般網站營運者唔使做嘢，但係管理解析器嘅人就要檢查清楚。

Android apps running native on macOS

https://github.com/kksimp/Mac.apk📅 2026-10-20 00:45 UTC+8▲ 92 ptsHN discussion

Mac.apk is an open-source tool that lets users run Android apps natively on Apple Silicon Macs without emulators, virtual machines, or a Linux layer underneath. Apps launch as ordinary macOS processes with native CPU and GPU performance, appearing in the Dock and behaving like regular Mac windows, though the software remains in alpha with variable app compatibility. It requires macOS 26.4 or newer and is free to use, with a commercial license available for companies.

Mac.apk 係一款開源軟件，可以喺 Apple Silicon Mac 上面原生運行 Android app，唔使模擬器、唔使虛擬機、亦唔使底層跑 Linux。app 會以普通 macOS 程序嘅方式啟動，用原生速度行 CPU 同 GPU，仲會喺 Dock 入面顯示同普通 Mac 視窗一樣操作，不過而家仲係 alpha 階段，有啲 app 未必完全支援。佢要求 macOS 26.4 或以上先至用到，個人使用完全免費。

https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154029📅 2026-10-20 00:00 UTC+8▲ 27 ptsHN discussion

The launch addresses a growing pain point as AI agents grow more complex and multi-step, where traditional monitoring tools fail to catch the subtle, implicit errors that actually matter to end users.

The industry's leading alternative, BHA, carries its own carcinogenic risks, leaving the aquaculture sector trapped in a cycle of trading one hazardous chemical for another.

魚粉係養魚飼料入面好重要嘅原料，但佢好容易自燃，由1950年代到1970年代引起咗至少90宗貨船火災，所以業界由1960年代開始用乙氧基喹啉嚟做阻燃劑。不過呢種化學品其實係致癌物，歐盟同英國已經禁用，但美國、澳洲、日本同南韓仲容許使用，而全球超過一半嘅海鮮都係養殖場出品，所以呢個問題直接影響到成個食物供應鏈。而家業界轉用嘅替代品BHA本身都有致癌風險，等於是換咗一個毒嚟代替另一個毒。

Galois Finite Fields and the Advanced Encryption Standard (AES)

https://www.cs.uaf.edu/2015/spring/cs463/lecture/03_23_AES.html📅 2026-10-17 16:34 UTC+8▲ 49 ptsHN discussion

This article explains how Galois Fields, specifically GF(2^8), provide the mathematical backbone of the Advanced Encryption Standard. It walks through how binary numbers map to polynomials, how addition in the field reduces to simple XOR, and how multiplication requires reduction by an irreducible polynomial like 0x11b. Understanding these operations reveals why AES achieves both strong security and efficient hardware implementation.

呢篇文章講咗 GF(2^8) 有限域點樣做 AES 加密嘅數學基礎，包括點樣將二進制數字對應到多項式、加法其實就係 XOR、乘法就要用一個不可約多項式做降冪。明白呢啲運算就知點解 AES 可以又安全又喺硬件上面行得快。

https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/quantum-computing/magic-state-cultivation-quantum-computing/📅 2026-10-16 02:00 UTC+8

Google Quantum AI has demonstrated that "magic state cultivation" works on its Willow processor, achieving a 40-fold reduction in errors and a record logical fidelity of 99.99%, as published in Science. Magic states are essential for universal quantum computing but have long been a severe resource bottleneck, with estimates suggesting up to 95% of a quantum computer's capacity could be consumed just creating them. This breakthrough turns a 20-year-old theoretical idea into experimental reality, marking a significant step toward building practical, error-corrected quantum computers.

Google Quantum AI 喺佢哋嘅 Willow 處理器上面成功示範咗「magic state cultivation」，將錯誤率降低咗 40 倍，邏輯保真度達到 99.99%，相關論文已經喺 Science 發表。Magic state 係實現通用量子計算嘅關鍵資源，但過去 20 年一直係最大嘅瓶頸之一，因為製造佢哋可能要佔用量子電腦 95% 嘅容量。呢個突破將一個理論概念變成咗實驗現實，係邁向實用化量子電腦嘅重要一步。

The core argument is that OAuth tokens and approval fields alone cannot guarantee a business action remains authorized at execution time, so a deterministic revalidation gate must run immediately before any irreversible side effect. A Vulnerability Is Not an Exposure

https://hackernoon.com/a-vulnerability-is-not-an-exposure📅 2026-10-19 21:27 UTC+8

The article argues that a vulnerability is not the same as an exposure, because exposure only exists when a working attack technique is demonstrated against a reachable target in a specific environment. With AI tools now finding thousands of vulnerabilities at scale while attackers use the same tools, the gap between discovery and remediation is widening, making it critical for security teams to track exposure as a distinct, time-sensitive state rather than conflating it with the mere presence of a flaw.

呢篇文章講嘅係，有漏洞唔等於有暴露，因為只有當攻擊者真係有方法、目標又真係可達嘅時候，先至算係一個暴露。而家AI已經可以大規模搵出幾千個漏洞，攻擊者用嘅都係同一啲工具，所以安全團隊一定要將「暴露」同「漏洞」分開管理，唔好一見CVE就當自己已經被暴露咗。

TorchNeuron: running HF models on AWS Trainium is easier than ever

https://huggingface.co/blog/hf-torchneuron📅 2026-10-19 08:00 UTC+8

Hugging Face has released TorchNeuron, a new native PyTorch backend for AWS Trainium chips that lets researchers and developers run and fine-tune models using the same Transformers, Diffusers, and TRL libraries they already use on GPUs. The plugin supports eager execution, torch.compile, and native distributed APIs like FSDP and tensor parallelism, with 89 percent of the top 100 text-generation model architectures supported out of the box, nearly double the coverage of the previous Optimum Neuron approach. The public beta is available from October 20, 2026, and marks a significant step toward making Trainium a first-class accelerator in the open-source AI ecosystem.

Hugging Face 出咗個叫 TorchNeuron 嘅新工具，係一個原生嘅 PyTorch backend 畀 AWS Trainium 呢啲 AI 晶片用，而家跑模型同微調都唔使再經 Optimum Neuron，直接用 Transformers、TRL 呢啲 library 就得，同喺 GPU 上面做嘢一樣。佢支援 eager mode、torch.compile 同埋原生嘅分佈式 API，頭 100 個最常用嘅文字生成模型架構有 89% 即刻支援，比之前多咗成倍。由 2026 年 10 月 20 號開始就有 public beta 用，對開發者嚟講真係方便咗好多。

The work matters because in data-sensitive settings like clinical or customer-support contexts, attributing a true fact to the wrong source can be as harmful as stating a false one.

Simon Willison shipped two updates to his LLM plugin ecosystem, releasing llm-tools-datasette 0.1.1 to fix a compatibility break caused by LLM 0.33's switch to httpx2, and llm-echo 0.5 which now accounts for tool calls, system prompts, and reasoning tokens when simulating token usage counts. llm-tools-datasette 0.1.1

https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/18/llm-tools-datasette/📅 2026-10-19 07:43 UTC+8

Simon Willison released version 0.1.1 of llm-tools-datasette, a plugin that exposes Datasette instances to LLM as a tool. The update fixes a compatibility issue that arose when LLM 0.33 switched to httpx2, which broke plugins that relied on httpx without declaring it as a direct dependency.

Simon Willison 出咗 llm-tools-datasette 0.1.1 版本，呢個 plugin 可以將 Datasette 實例暴露畀 LLM 做工具用。之前 LLM 0.33 轉咗用 httpx2，搞到冇直接依賴 httpx 嘅 plugin 全部壞咗，呢次就係修返呢個問題。

66 words

llm-echo 0.5

https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/18/llm-echo/📅 2026-10-19 07:16 UTC+8

Simon Willison has released version 0.5 of llm-echo, a debug plugin for his LLM command-line tool that simulates a model by simply echoing back whatever prompt it receives, without calling any real language model. The update improves the accuracy of simulated token usage counts by now factoring in tool calls, system prompts, and reasoning tokens. This is a useful utility for developers who need to test and debug LLM workflows without incurring API costs or waiting for actual model responses.

Profiling web-edit with Python 3.15: flame graphs and source heatmaps

https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/12/python-3-15-web-edit-profiling/📅 2026-10-13 00:58 UTC+8

Simon Willison used Python 3.15's new built-in profiling.sampling module to profile his web-edit application, generating flame graphs and source heatmaps with the help of GPT-6 Astra Extra High running in Codex. The profiling revealed that mixed line-ending preservation dominated the workload, with preserve_endings appearing in 64.8% of recorded CPU stacks and SequenceMatcher.find_longest_match contributing 38.3% of leaf samples. The exercise also highlighted the importance of sampling all threads, as 95.1% of stacks originated from AnyIO's worker thread and would have been missed by a main-thread-only profile.

Simon Willison 用咗 Python 3.15 新出嘅內置 profiling.sampling 模組去 profile 佢嘅 web-edit 程式，仲用 GPT-6 Astra Extra High 喺 Codex 入面跑，整咗 flame graph 同 source heatmap 出嚟。結果發現混合行尾保留（preserve_endings）佔咗 64.8% 嘅 CPU stack，而 SequenceMatcher.find_longest_match 就佔咗 38.3% 嘅 leaf samples。佢亦都強調一定要 sampling 所有 thread，因為 95.1% 嘅 stack 都係由 AnyIO 嘅 worker thread 出嚟，淨係 profile 主 thread 嘅話根本睇唔到。

https://www.anthropic.com/news/2026-us-midterms📅 2026-10-15 02:33 UTC+8

AI by Aakash 20 articles

Reverse Engineer Anything.

https://www.aibyaakash.com/p/reverse-engineer-anything📅 2026-10-18 13:52 UTC+8

Replit CEO Amjad Masad flagged a growing trend of people using AI to reverse engineer software products, and a GitHub repo called Reverse Engineer Anything has exploded to 310K stars by letting users decompile and rebuild apps with plain English prompts. The author tested the tool by reading Signal's desktop app and rebuilding Linear's core issue tracker in under four minutes, confirming that even a $2.5 billion product can be cloned with minimal effort. This raises a fundamental question about what competitive moats remain in software when AI can read, understand, and replicate codebases at near-zero cost.

The release has drawn both awe and skepticism, with experts noting that some results may not survive independent scrutiny and that roughly 20% are disproofs or counterexamples.

Ontology-Driven Observability: Building an End-to-End Knowledge Graph at Netflix Scale

https://netflixtechblog.com/ontology-driven-observability-building-an-end-to-end-knowledge-graph-at-netflix-scale-421392cf5adf📅 2026-10-17 00:01 UTC+8

Netflix engineers have built an ontology-driven knowledge graph to solve the problem of slow incident investigations at their massive scale, where disconnected tools disagree on what entities even are. By encoding their operational world into machine-readable triples using W3C standards and storing them in a custom RDF graph database called QuipuDB, they turned a four-hour human bottleneck into a single queryable source of truth. The system also converts the unstructured reasoning in incident Slack channels into structured facts, preventing institutional knowledge from evaporating after each incident.

Netflix 的工程團隊為咗解決佢哋超大规模系統入面事故排查太慢嘅問題，起咗一個基於本體論嘅知識圖譜，將散落在唔同工具入面嘅數據統一成機器可讀嘅三元組。佢哋用 W3C 標準將應用、事故、部署呢啲實體嘅關係寫入自家開發嘅圖數據庫 QuipuDB，將原本要四粒鐘先搵到根因嘅人工排查變成一條 SPARQL 查詢就搞掂。呢個系統仲會將事故 Slack 頻道入面嘅討論轉化成結構化事實，防止團隊經驗隨住頻道沉寂而消失。

Netflix has detailed how it integrates its large-scale Foundation Model into production personalization systems through three distinct approaches: embeddings, subgraph integration, and direct fine-tuning. Each method offers different trade-offs between ease of adoption, computational cost, and the ability to leverage the full power of the model, with embeddings serving as the most accessible entry point and subgraph integration reserved for high-impact use cases. The article fills a notable gap in the literature by providing practical guidance on deploying large transformer-based models within existing production pipelines at scale.

The platform's API-first, graph-first architecture and its use of analytical cubes as a consumption protocol now serve as a foundation for Netflix's broader data infrastructure.

https://netflixtechblog.com/measuring-the-impact-of-personalized-recommendations-4c26be3a4d96📅 2026-10-15 03:57 UTC+8

Netflix researchers have developed a new model to measure the true causal value of personalized recommendations, addressing the core challenge that recommendations are inherently targeted to existing demand and cannot be simply credited for the viewing they influence. Using a low-rank choice model that separates member preferences from recommendation effects, combined with small-scale randomized experiments for validation, the study estimates how recommendations change both total viewing and which specific titles members choose. The work offers a rigorous framework for understanding how much value personalization creates beyond what members would have watched anyway.

TimeSeries Tiered Storage Journey: Kafka/Flink Streams to Native Cassandra Cold Reads

https://netflixtechblog.com/timeseries-tiered-storage-journey-kafka-flink-streams-to-native-cassandra-cold-reads-e59d597c9d60📅 2026-10-15 03:56 UTC+8

Netflix engineers developed two tiered storage solutions for their TimeSeries platform, which manages multiple petabytes of temporal event data, to cut infrastructure costs by migrating older, infrequently accessed data from expensive hot storage to cheaper S3 object storage while preserving a consistent query API. The first approach streams data to S3 via Apache Kafka and Flink with Parquet compaction for optimized reads, while the second takes a more radical path by reading directly from existing Cassandra backups in S3 using a custom ReadOnlyCompactionStrategy that eliminates redundant data transfer and reduces operational complexity. Together these solutions deliver sub-second cold reads at scale and significantly shrink Cassandra's on-disk footprint across Netflix's largest datasets.

Netflix 嘅 TimeSeries 平台要處理幾 PB 嘅時間序列數據，為咗慳錢佢哋搞咗兩套方案將舊數據由貴嘅熱儲存遷移去 S3 冷儲存，同時保持查詢介面不變。第一套方案用 Kafka 同 Flink 將數據串流寫入 S3 再經 Parquet 壓縮優化讀取，第二套就大膽啲直接讀 Cassandra 本身已經備份喺 S3 嘅數據，用一個新嘅 ReadOnlyCompactionStrategy 嚟減少重複傳輸同運維負擔。兩套方案都做到亞秒級嘅冷數據查詢，大幅縮減咗 Cassandra 嘅磁碟用量同整體基礎設施開支。

Netflix rebuilt the enrichment step of its Ads Event Publisher, replacing a database-based Metadata Registry with a stateful streaming join in Apache Flink to handle the extreme traffic spikes of live events like NFL games and the Jake Paul vs. Mike Tyson fight. The old design required synchronous database writes on the ad-serving critical path, which created scaling bottlenecks, single points of failure, and event freshness risks at live scale. The new architecture removes that dependency entirely, allowing the pipeline to scale elastically with live viewership while maintaining strict SLAs for downstream consumers like frequency capping and billing.

Netflix 將廣告事件管道入面嘅 enrichment 步驟由資料庫查詢改為用 Apache Flink 做即時串流 join，解決咗直播賽事例如 NFL 同 Jake Paul 對 Mike Tyson 拳賽帶嚟嘅流量爆發問題。舊設計要喺廣告投放嘅關鍵路徑上同步寫入資料庫，一旦直播觀眾衝上幾千萬就會變成瓶頸，仲有單點故障同延遲風險。新架構將資料庫依賴完全移除，令到成條管道可以跟住直播流量彈性擴展，同時確保下游系統例如 frequency capping 同計費都唔會受影響。

Running Apache Spark experiments in my sleep (and on a plane)

https://netflixtechblog.com/running-apache-spark-experiments-in-my-sleep-and-on-a-plane-588816b6b5f8📅 2026-10-15 03:49 UTC+8

A Netflix engineer spent two months debugging a critical Spark pipeline that processes tens of billions of hourly events, ultimately discovering that running many experiments in parallel through branch isolation was far more effective than optimizing single runs. The root cause turned out to be memory bloat from collect_list operations, Scala Option boxing, and bloated string attributes inflating individual event weight, and fixing these reduced peak executor memory from 112% to 58% of the slot. The work also revealed counterintuitive Spark behaviours, such as narrower rows triggering faster OOMs by pushing tables below the auto-broadcast threshold.

Netflix一位工程師花咗兩個月調試一條處理數以百億計事件嘅Spark數據管道，最終發現同時跑多個實驗比優化單次運行有效得多，佢仲用咗AI agent同tmux喺飛機上都能夠繼續迭代。根本原因係collect_list操作、Scala Option boxing同埋過重嘅字串屬性令單個事件體積膨脹，解決之後峰值內存從112%降到58%。佢仲發現咗一個違反直覺嘅現象：將行變窄反而會令OOM更快發生，因為會觸發Spark用broadcast join而將成張表釘喺內存入面。

Netflix engineers built an automated system called the Stratum Resource Tuner to right-size containers on its internal media processing platform, eliminating the need for manual resource configuration. https://www.deeplearning.ai/the-batch/issue-375📅 2026-10-16 22:00 UTC+8

Andrew Ng argues that the gap between AI's performance on verifiable tasks like coding and math versus judgement tasks like business strategy is far wider than commonly understood, driven by the fact that reinforcement learning and supervised learning techniques work far more effectively when ground-truth answers exist. He warns that society has a pattern of overgeneralizing AI's capabilities from successes on verifiable benchmarks, and notes that humans will retain a context advantage on judgement-heavy work for the foreseeable future. The issue also covers Anthropic's launch of Claude Haiku 5.5 and Claude Sonnet 5.5, which show strong benchmark results but at higher token costs than competitors.

Andrew Ng 指出 AI 喺可驗證任務（例如寫程式、做數學）同需要判斷力嘅任務（例如商業策略、處理客訴）之間嘅表現差距，比大部分人想像中大好多，因為強化學習同監督學習呢啲技術喺有標準答案嘅情況下先至特別有效。佢提醒社會好容易因為 AI 喺 benchmark 上面嘅成績就過度推斷佢嘅能力，而人類喺需要大量上下文判斷嘅工作上短期內仲係有優勢。呢期亦提到 Anthropic 推出咗 Claude Haiku 5.5 同 Claude Sonnet 5.5，跑分唔錯但 token 成本比對手貴。

Issue 374

https://www.deeplearning.ai/the-batch/issue-374📅 2026-10-09 22:00 UTC+8

Andrew Ng argues that the massive data center build-outs needed to support agentic AI workflows are being widely underestimated, as agents generate far more compute, storage, and networking demand than human users ever could. Staying Evergreen: How we automated secure access and repo hygiene in a shared org

https://github.blog/engineering/platform-security/staying-evergreen-how-we-automated-secure-access-and-repo-hygiene-in-a-shared-org/📅 2026-10-16 02:00 UTC+8

GitHub built Evergreen, a GitHub App that automates quarterly access reviews for outside collaborators and archives stale repositories in a shared organization with over 1,400 employees. The app uses only native GitHub features like scheduled actions, webhooks, and issues, tracks all state as JSON files in a git repo so the commit history doubles as an audit trail, and follows a fail-secure principle where unanswered reviews result in access revocation rather than leaving permissions open. It matters because it turns a tedious, error-prone manual chore into a self-service workflow that feels considerate to users while maintaining strict security defaults.

GitHub 內部開發咗一個叫 Evergreen 嘅 GitHub App，用嚟自動處理共享組織入面外來協作者嘅季度權限審查同埋將長期冇活動嘅 repo 歸檔。佢完全用 GitHub 原生功能去實作，所有狀態以 JSON 檔案存在 git repo 入面，commit 歷史本身就係審計紀錄，而且遵循 fail-secure 原則，無人回應就預設撤銷權限。呢個做法將原本繁瑣容易出錯嘅手動管理工作變成咗一個對用戶友善但安全預設嚴格嘅自動化流程。

From 2,500 requests to 24: Rebuilding star history without the stargazers

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/from-2500-requests-to-24-rebuilding-star-history-without-the-stargazers/📅 2026-10-15 03:11 UTC+8

GitHub has introduced a new API endpoint that returns daily star counts for repositories, replacing the old method that required thousands of individual stargazer requests to reconstruct growth curves. The change came after GitHub restricted access to stargazer lists to prevent identity harvesting, which broke tools like Star History and prompted a collaboration between GitHub engineers and the community to build a purpose-built solution. The new endpoint filters stars by time range before aggregating, cutting requests for popular repositories like react/react from roughly 2,511 down to about 24 while also reducing database load.

GitHub 推出咗一個新嘅 API endpoint，可以直接攞到倉庫每日嘅 star 數，取代咗以前要逐頁抓取幾千條 stargazer 記錄先至可以重建增長曲線嘅做法。呢個改動係因為 GitHub 為咗防止有人利用 stargazer 名單做垃圾郵件同釣魚攻擊而限制咗存取權限，結果搞到 Star History 呢類工具用唔到，最後 GitHub 同社群合作設計咗呢個新方案。新 endpoint 會先按時間範圍篩選再聚合，令 react/react 呢類熱門倉庫由原本大約 2,511 次請求減到只需要 24 次，同時大幅降低資料庫嘅負擔。

How we make AI coding more cost efficient without sacrificing task quality

https://github.blog/ai-and-ml/github-copilot/how-we-make-ai-coding-more-cost-efficient-without-sacrificing-task-quality/📅 2026-09-03 02:00 UTC+8

GitHub Copilot engineers found that reducing tokens per individual tool call often backfires, because the agent then has to repeat work and spend more tokens overall, so they shifted their optimization target to end-to-end task cost instead. They shipped three changes — a selective output compressor that strips repetitive build and test noise while preserving source-like content, removal of unused line-number prefixes from file reads, and a meta-prompting loop that halved a task-tool prompt — each validated through offline benchmarks and online experiments before release. The core lesson is that efficiency gains must be measured across the full task from user request to final result, and any behavior worth preserving needs an explicit regression test.

GitHub Copilot 嘅工程師發現，淨係減少每次工具呼叫嘅 token 數往往會弄巧反拙，因為 AI agent 要重新做返啲嘢，結果整體反而用多咗 token，所以佢哋將優化目標改做成個 task 嘅端到端成本。佢哋推出咗三個改動——一個選擇性壓縮器，只係刪走重複嘅 build 同 test 輸出而保留有用內容、移除咗讀檔時冇用嘅行號前綴、同埋用 meta-prompting 將一個 task tool 嘅 prompt 縮短咗一半——每個改動都經過離線 benchmark 同線上實驗先至上線。最核心嘅教訓係，效率提升一定要由用戶提出請求到最終完成成個 task 去衡量，而任何值得保留嘅行為都要有明確嘅回歸測試。

https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/architecture/multi-region-disaster-recovery-for-amazon-api-gateway-websocket-apis/📅 2026-10-14 22:31 UTC+8

This article presents a design pattern for multi-region disaster recovery of Amazon API Gateway WebSocket APIs, combining Route 53 failover routing, DynamoDB Global Tables for cross-region state replication, and a canary health probe to automatically reconnect clients and restore session state when a regional outage occurs. The approach matters because WebSocket connections are persistent and tied to a single region, meaning a regional failure severs every active connection and destroys shared application state for real-time workloads such as live auctions, collaborative editors, and IoT dashboards.

呢篇文章介紹咗一個用 Amazon API Gateway WebSocket API 做跨區域災難恢復嘅方案，核心做法係用 Route 53 做 DNS 故障轉移、用 DynamoDB Global Tables 將應用狀態即時複製到第二個區域，再配合一個 canary 健康探測器自動偵測故障並觸發切換。呢個方案之所以重要，係因為 WebSocket 連線係持久且綁死喺單一區域嘅，一旦嗰個區域出事，所有連線即刻斷晒，用戶嘅會話狀態都會一併消失，對實時競投、協作編輯器呢類應用嚟講影響極大。

ReadyOn’s Four Walls of tenant isolation on Amazon EKS

https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/architecture/readyons-four-walls-of-tenant-isolation-on-amazon-eks/📅 2026-09-19 05:33 UTC+8

ReadyOn, an AWS Partner serving Fortune 100 enterprises, built a four-layer tenant isolation architecture on Amazon EKS to protect sensitive workforce data including payroll and organizational hierarchies. The "Four Walls" model combines Kubernetes namespaces with GitOps-enforced RBAC, Karpenter-managed dedicated node pools using a dual-taint strategy, per-tenant VPC security groups, and dedicated Amazon Aurora databases, requiring an attacker to simultaneously breach all four independent layers to cross a tenant boundary. This approach addresses the fundamental limitation that Kubernetes namespaces were never designed as a security boundary, which is critical for multi-tenant platforms handling regulated enterprise data.

ReadyOn 係一家服務財富100強企業嘅 AWS 合作夥伴，佢哋喺 Amazon EKS 上面設計咗一套「四道牆」租戶隔離架構，用嚟保護薪酬數據同組織架構呢啲極敏感嘅企業資料。四道牆分別係 Kubernetes namespace 配合 GitOps 強制 RBAC、Karpenter 管理嘅專用節點池、租戶專屬嘅 VPC 安全組，同埋獨立嘅 Amazon Aurora 資料庫，攻擊者要同時突破四層先至可以跨租戶存取資料。呢個做法解決咗 Kubernetes namespace 本身唔係安全邊界嘅根本問題，對於處理受監管企業數據嘅多租戶平台嚟講非常關鍵。

Recent Developments in LLM Architectures: KV Sharing, mHC, and Compressed Attention

https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/recent-developments-in-llm-architectures📅 2026-05-16 19:33 UTC+8

Recent open-weight LLM releases are converging on a common architectural theme: reducing KV cache size and memory traffic to make long-context inference feasible for reasoning models and agent workflows. The article walks through specific design choices including cross-layer KV sharing and per-layer embeddings in Gemma 4, compressed convolutional attention in ZAYA1, attention budgeting in Laguna XS.2, and mHC with compressed attention in DeepSeek V4, showing how each tackles the growing bottleneck of attention computation and memory at scale. These are not incremental tweaks but increasingly intricate structural changes inside the transformer block that signal a shift in how the field approaches efficiency.

最近好多開源大模型都集中喺同一個方向，就係點樣縮細 KV cache 同減少記憶體流量，因為而家推理模型同 agent workflow 都要處理好長嘅 context，舊有嘅 attention 機制已經頂唔順。篇文章逐個拆解咗 Gemma 4 嘅跨層 KV 共享同 per-layer embeddings、ZAYA1 嘅壓縮卷積 attention、Laguna XS.2 嘅 attention budgeting，同埋 DeepSeek V4 嘅 mHC 加壓縮 attention，講清楚每個設計點樣喺 transformer 結構入面做手腳嚟慳記憶體同算力。呢啲唔係小修小補，而係反映成個行業已經開始重新設計 transformer 內部結構去應付長 context 嘅瓶頸。

From GPT-2 to gpt-oss: Analyzing the Architectural Advances

https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/from-gpt-2-to-gpt-oss-analyzing-the📅 2025-08-09 19:23 UTC+8

OpenAI has released gpt-oss-120b and gpt-oss-20b, their first open-weight large language models since GPT-2 in 2019, and a detailed architectural analysis reveals that while the core transformer design remains unchanged, years of incremental refinements such as the removal of dropout, adoption of RoPE positional encoding, and the switch from GELU to Swish activations have collectively driven substantial progress. The models can run locally on consumer hardware thanks to MXFP4 quantization, making them accessible to researchers and developers outside major labs. This release matters because it offers a rare transparent look at how leading AI labs have converged on similar architectural choices and where the real gains have come from, namely data and algorithmic tweaks rather than fundamental design shifts.

OpenAI 終於推出咗 gpt-oss-120b 同 gpt-oss-20b，係佢哋自 2019 年 GPT-2 之後首次開放權重嘅大模型，文章詳細分析咗由 GPT-2 到 gpt-oss 之間嘅架構演進，包括移除 dropout、改用 RoPE 位置編碼、以 Swish 取代 GELU 等等，雖然核心 transformer 架構冇根本改變，但呢啲逐步改良累積落嚟效果好顯著。呢啲模型靠 MXFP4 量化技術可以喺普通顯卡上跑，對研究人員同開發者嚟講好有價值，亦反映出現時頂級 AI 實驗室嘅進步主要靠數據同演算法微調，而唔係架構上嘅大突破。
